{"parentUuid":"6e188f87-b8ef-4851-b06f-b5c0b59bdfa6","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"54d15614-e730-4283-9a5e-12e6505d77b7","timestamp":"2026-05-05T15:10:04.326Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"54d15614-e730-4283-9a5e-12e6505d77b7","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- empirical-probe-first: Probe ALL viable strategies in a multi-option ladder BEFORE committing to one. Use when a session-plan / decision-ADR / spec presents ≥3 strategy options (e.g. \"Strategy A1/A2/A3\" or \"alternative B/C/D\") and the recommended option's source-evidence may be stale. Outputs a probe matrix per strategy + commit decision with empirical citation. Pairs with `vendor-api-probe.sh` (deterministic detection layer; SessionStart hook) and `decompose-work` (deterministic-vs-LLM split per probe). Charter promotion target — codifies L-S32-1 doctrine surfaced at S32 Track A pivot (master-plan recommended A2 vnstock alternate; empirical probe rejected A1+A2; A3 SSI direct httpx adopted instead).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. {"parentUuid":"65ac7f88-736e-46dc-9de1-b14bb5722d29","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7f5d15b0-0285-49c9-836a-9fed59534dfa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GjqijhvSTSfijr8cUSSX1n","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# PROJECT CHARTER\n2\t## StockForge — Personal Investment Advisor for Vietnamese Stock Market\n3\t\n4\t> **Status**: Immutable v1.0 — changes require explicit charter revision.\n5\t> **Scope of immutability**: Vision, principles, success criteria.\n6\t> **Things that do evolve**: Agent configs, specs, implementation, signal weights, eval sets.\n7\t\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t## Vision\n11\t\n12\tAn AI-first investment research and advisory system that combines **quant fund methodology** with **Vietnam-specific edge sources** (KOL networks, crowd sentiment, pump pattern detection) to surface high-conviction opportunities in an inefficient market where logic-based screening alone is insufficient.\n13\t\n14\tPrimary user: project owner (self) + 3-5 trusted peers in Vietnamese investing community.\n15\t\n16\tPrimary goal: build a system that compounds edge over time through proprietary data accumulation, not through any single clever algorithm. After 12-24 months of dogfood, the system should be a genuine advisor I would pay money for if someone else built it.\n17\t\n18\tNot a goal: high-frequency trading, replacing human judgment, becoming a SaaS in Year 1, beating frontier LLMs at general reasoning, providing licensed financial advice.\n19\t\n20\t---\n21\t\n22\t## The Core Insight (the why)\n23\t\n24\t**Quant funds (Renaissance, Two Sigma, DE Shaw) win in efficient markets through clean data + execution speed + diverse alpha factors.** They don't use sentiment from Facebook/YouTube because at their scale it's noise, not signal — and in efficient markets it gets arbitraged away anyway.\n25\t\n26\t**Vietnam stock market inverts this.** It is structurally inefficient:\n27\t- 85-90% retail investor share (vs 20-25% in US)\n28\t- Information asymmetry between sophisticated and retail crowd\n29\t- KOL/influencer recommendations measurably move prices\n30\t- \"Đội lái\" (pump operators) operate openly\n31\t- News and narrative cycles dominate over fundamentals for months at a time\n32\t- Foreign flow creates non-fundamental price pressure\n33\t\n34\tIn this market, **sentiment + influence + narrative tracking is signal, not noise**. And LLMs can process Vietnamese unstructured text at scale that was impossible before 2023.\n35\t\n36\tThis creates a **structural arbitrage**: apply quant methodology + LLM reasoning + sentiment/influence data layers that no commercial product offers for VN. Position not as \"decision maker\" but as **advisor + alert watcher + bias mirror**.\n37\t\n38\t---\n39\t\n40\t## The Craft Philosophy\n41\t\n42\tTake the craft seriously. Build for personal use first, commercial second.\n43\t\n44\t- Production-grade engineering standards from Day 1 (DDD + clean architecture + multi-loop discipline)\n45\t- Commercial extension kept as option, never as driver\n46\t- When self-use conflicts with hypothetical-future-SaaS, self-use wins\n47\t- Quality bar: I genuinely trust output enough to act on real money decisions\n48\t- Edge comes from compounding moats (proprietary data, calibration, personal bias log), not from any single feature\n49\t\n50\tReference models: Renaissance Technologies (data + iteration discipline), Bridgewater (principles-based decision making), personal Bloomberg Terminal (data density + analysis depth), Athletic.com (subscription-quality content for niche audience).\n51\t\n52\t---\n53\t\n54\t## Core Principles (non-negotiable)\n55\t\n56\t1. **Evidence grounding** — every claim, every number traceable to source + as-of date. Hallucination in finance = bug, not stylistic issue.\n57\t\n58\t2. **Structured output over narrative** — multi-criteria assessment, never single \"buy/sell\" score. Output is trade-off matrix.\n59\t\n60\t3. **Adversarial by design** — bear + bull + critic + quant + behavior + manager perspectives. Never consensus from single agent.\n61\t\n62\t4. **Proprietary data moat** — every news ingested, every KOL recommendation tracked, every thesis post-mortem'd compounds the edge. Raw data is sacred.\n63\t\n64\t5. **Pattern transfer + local adaptation** — global setups (cyclical bottom, dividend value, GARP) + Vietnam-specific overlays (policy timing, foreign flow, narrative phase).\n65\t\n66\t6. **Human-in-loop is the product** — augment thinking, never replace it. System should challenge user's gut instinct without overriding it.\n67\t\n68\t7. **Dogfood mandatory** — if I don't use it weekly for real decisions, the feature gets killed. Self-use is the only validation that matters in Year 1.\n69\t\n70\t8. **Calibration over confidence** — system tracks its own accuracy across signal types, never claims confidence it hasn't earned through hit rate.\n71\t\n72\t9. **No LLM math** — LLM never generates numbers. All calculations through deterministic code with verified data inputs. LLM only interprets.\n73\t\n74\t10. **Position sizing & risk management are deterministic** — code-enforced rules, LLM cannot override. Maximum position size, sector concentration, stop loss rules are immutable per session.\n75\t\n76\t---\n77\t\n78\t## The Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n79\t\n80\tSystem operates on four distinct signal tiers, each with different lead time, reliability, and processing approach:\n81\t\n82\t**Tier 1 — Hard Data (deterministic):** prices, volumes, fundamentals, foreign flow, macro indicators. Zero LLM, pure code. Lead time: hours-days.\n83\t\n84\t**Tier 2 — Official Narrative (semi-structured):** mainstream financial news, broker reports, official company disclosures, government announcements. LLM extracts claims + classifies sentiment + detects contradictions. Lead time: real-time.\n85\t\n86\t**Tier 3 — Influence Network (semi-structured):** YouTube channels, Facebook KOL pages, Telegram channels, podcasts. LLM transcribes + extracts recommendations + tags entities. Calibration database tracks each KOL's accuracy over time. Lead time: can lead by days.\n87\t\n88\t**Tier 4 — Crowd Sentiment & Pump Detection (unstructured):** community forums, public chat groups, comments. LLM classifies sentiment + detects coordinated posting + identifies pump narratives + tracks narrative lifecycle phase. Lead time: typically lags price (signals exhaustion).\n89\t\n90\tThe combination — when Tier 1+2+3 agree before Tier 4 heats up = early opportunity; when Tier 4 hot but Tier 1+2 don't support = likely top — is the core analytical edge.\n91\t\n92\tSee `specs/tier1-strategic/001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md` for detailed tier specs.\n93\t\n94\t---\n95\t\n96\t## First Sub-Scope (locked 6 months)\n97\t\n98\t**Vietnam stocks listed on HOSE/HNX/UPCoM**, specifically:\n99\t- VN30 + mid-cap (vốn hóa 2,000-50,000 tỷ VND) for primary coverage\n100\t- Long-only equity strategies (no derivatives, no shorting)\n101\t- Holding periods: 1-month minimum, 6-24 month preferred\n102\t- Position sizing per stock: 5-15% of portfolio\n103\t\n104\tOut of scope (Year 1): derivatives, warrants (CW), short selling, cryptocurrency, fund products, foreign markets, day trading, automated execution.\n105\t\n106\t---\n107\t\n108\t## Honest Boundaries (what system does NOT do)\n109\t\n110\t- **Predict price** — system predicts narrative phase, money flow direction, mispricing level. Never a price target with false precision.\n111\t- **Replace fundamental research** — surfaces opportunities, user must verify thesis before acting.\n112\t- **Execute trades** — pure research/alert tool. Execution stays manual.\n113\t- **Provide licensed financial advice** — personal research aid only. Output explicitly framed as \"thesis exploration\", not \"buy/sell recommendation\".\n114\t- **Time the market precisely** — works on phase identification (early/mid/late), not on tick-level timing.\n115\t- **Insider information** — uses only public sources. No paid leaks, no insider channels, no anything that crosses legal/ethical lines.\n116\t\n117\t---\n118\t\n119\t## Success Criteria\n120\t\n121\t**Month 3 (end of Phase 1 — Foundation):**\n122\t- Tier 1 + 2 data pipeline operational for VN30\n123\t- 50 companies have basic dossier in wiki\n124\t- First 5 thesis recorded with formal structure\n125\t- Can run /thesis-validate on a stock end-to-end in <5 minutes\n126\t\n127\t**Month 6 (end of Phase 2 — Edge Sources):**\n128\t- 30+ KOL channels tracked, calibration database has >100 recommendations with outcomes pending\n129\t- Tier 3 + 4 ingestion Phase 3 — Multi-perspective):**\n134\t- Bear/bull/critic/quant/macro/behavior agents operational\n135\t- Pattern library has 15+ Vietnam-specific patterns with outcome data\n136\t- Personal bias log shows 3+ identified biases\n137\t- Thesis hit rate measurable (statistically meaningful sample)\n138\t\n139\t**Month 12 (end of Phase 4 — Compounding):**\n140\t- Karpathy outer loop optimizing signal on dogfood portfolio (or honest accounting of why not)\n144\t\n145\t**Ultimate signal of success**: When my gut says \"buy XYZ\", I check what StockForge says first — and seriously consider it when it embeddings** — text-embedding-3-small (Vietnamese acceptable)\n158\t- **NestJS** — only if/when we need a public-facing API (Phase 4+)\n159\t- **Streamlit/Next.js** — Streamlit for personal dashboard (Phase 1-2), Next.js if SaaS path\n160\t- **Apache Airflow / Prefect** — data pipeline orchestration\n161\t\n162\t**Architecture:**\n163\t- Clean Architecture with DDD tactical patterns (same as Domain-Driven (DDD) + selective BDD\n171\t- Sandwich pattern (Architect → Dev → Verifier) from Day 1\n172\t- Verify-Before-Write (VBW) protocol mandatory\n173\t- Three-tier quality gates (deterministic / probabilistic / human)\n174\t\n175\t---\n176\t\n177\t## Data Sources (Tier 1 budget)\n178\t\n179\t**Free/cheap (Phase 1):**\n180\t- vnstock (open source) — historical EOD, fundamentals\n181\t- Vietstock public — financial reports\n182\t- TCBS API — semi-public market data\n183\t- CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz — news scraping\n184\t- Public YouTube channels — transcript via We win on integration + edge sources, not on raw analysis depth.\n252\t- **Build for general retail public** — we build for ourselves and 3-5 peers. Mass market needs hand-holding we won't provide.\n253\t- **Trust LLM for numbers** — every numerical output must come from code with traceable inputs.\n254\t- **Build features without dogfooding** — speculative features die fast in this project.\n255\t- **Optimize for backtest performance** — Goodhart's Law is real. We optimize for forward decision quality.\n256\t- **Make money fast** — this is a 24-month project minimum to reach edge. Maximum position size, sector concentration, stop loss rules are immutable per session.\n\n---\n\n## The Four-Tier Signal Architecture\n\nSystem operates on four distinct signal tiers, each with different lead time, reliability, and processing approach:\n\n**Tier 1 — Hard Data (deterministic):** prices, volumes, fundamentals, foreign flow, macro indicators. This charter stays stable.\n\n---\n\n## Document Index\n\n| Document | Purpose | Stability |\n|---|---|---|\n| `PROJECT_CHARTER.md` (this) | Vision + principles | Immutable for ~3 months |\n| `AGENT_OPERATING_MANUAL.md` | Agent configs, skills, workflows | Living, frequent updates |\n| `SPEC_TEMPLATE.md` | How to write specs | Stable |\n| `specs/tier1-strategic/001-four-tier-signal-architecture.md` | Strategic: signal architecture | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/001-validate-investment-thesis.md` | First implementation spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/002-influence-network-tracking.md` | KOL tracking system spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/003-crowd-sentiment-pump-detection.md` | Pump detection spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/004-multi-perspective-adversarial-agents.md` | Adversarial analysis spec | Stable |\n| `specs/tier2-feature/005-karpathy-outer-loop.md` | Pipeline self-optimization spec | Stable |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/project.md` | Current state | Continuously updated |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules | Append-mostly |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/` | All thesis ever recorded | Append-only |\n| `agent-workspace/memory/post-mortems/` | Thesis outcome reviews | Append-only |\n| `agent-workspace/calibration/` | KOL accuracy, signal calibration data | Continuously updated |\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Charter (what we explicitly reject)\n\nTo stay focused, we reject these tempting paths:\n\n- **Predict prices with ML models** — models trained on VN price data overfit; no edge there.\n- **Compete with broker research** — we have less data, less staff. We win on integration + edge sources, not on raw analysis depth.\n- **Build for general retail public** — we build for ourselves and 3-5 peers. Mass market needs hand-holding we won't provide.\n- **Trust LLM for numbers** — every numerical output must come from code with traceable inputs.\n- **Build features without dogfooding** — speculative features die fast in this project.\n- **Optimize for backtest performance** — Goodhart's Law is real. We optimize for forward decision quality.\n- **Make money fast** — this is a 24-month project minimum to reach edge. Quick wins are anti-pattern.\n","numLines":257,"startLine":1,"totalLines":257}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"65ac7f88-736e-46dc-9de1-b14bb5722d29","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

KG migration only if Obsidian text-search becomes inadequate for cross-entity thesis synthesis. To be codified in `proposals/autonomous-protocol.md` § Self-detect-drift mechanism during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E2 — Agent-Notes Promotion Frequency\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 OR S8 Track 9 → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: How often does agent-notes → skill/hook/deterministic promotion run?\n- **options**: A: Phase-boundary only (manual review by promotion subagent, opus, fresh ctx) / B: Per-session-end Stop hook scans new agent-notes; dispatches subagent if ≥3 similar entries / C: Continuous — every new note triggers similarity check / D: open answer\n- **answer**: A (Recommended) — Phase-boundary only; manual review by promotion subagent. To be codified in `proposals/decision-discipline.md` § Promotion frequency during S16 IMPL.\n\n### Q-E3 — Promotion Target Priority\n\n- **fire_when**: S5 Track 7 OR S8 Track 9 (paired with E2) → fired S15 PLAN 2026-04-29\n- **status**: closed\n- **fired_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1)\n- **closed_at**: 2026-04-29 (S15 PLAN Batch 1, same turn)\n- **source**: bundle 002 cluster E\n- **question**: When agent-note becomes promotable, where does it land?\n- **options**: A: New skill in `.claude/skills/<name>/` / B: New hook script in `scripts/hooks/` / C: Update existing constitution file / D: All three valid; promotion subagent decides per pattern type — but priority MUST be hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST (cheapest first) / E: open answer\n- **answer**: D (Recommended; rephrased as \"Hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST\") — cheapest deterministic check first; skill encodes procedural discipline; charter amendment heaviest. {"parentUuid":"07a8f71d-0893-4b0c-b1ed-da2e59a3afe0","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` — agent writes pending; either human or agent (per Auto-mv rule below) moves resolved bundles to answered/\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\n**Auto-mv rule (HH-E.2 — D-031 ratification, 2026-05-05)**: Agent MAY mv a bundle from `q-and-a/pending/` to `q-and-a/answered/` IFF ALL of the following hold:\n\n1. **Frontmatter signal**: bundle frontmatter `status:` field value starts with one of: `answered-`, `closed-`, `resolved-`. Examples already in repo: `answered-via-chat`, `answered-via-AskUserQuestion`, `answered-2026-05-04-via-chat`. Detection is deterministic (head -20 of file + grep `^status:`).\n2. **No human-veto signal**: bundle frontmatter has NO `wait_until:` ISO-8601 timestamp greater than current epoch — if present, agent MUST defer mv until that timestamp passes. Allows human to override auto-mv per-bundle without contract amendment.\n3. **No global pause**: file `human-workspace/q-and-a/.auto-mv-paused` does NOT exist — global kill switch for the auto-mv mechanism (single empty file presence pauses all auto-mv).\n4. **Hook validation**: the mv is performed by `scripts/hooks/qa-pending-auto-mover.sh` (Stop hook). Direct manual `mv` invocation by agent (e.g. via Bash tool) is STILL forbidden — only the validated hook path is authorized.\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` write channel + the auto-mv rule above + `notifications/` write channel (existing).\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"8db26bd7-da13-4de1-a9a1-11b4f0d16673","timestamp":"2026-05-05T15:10:16.589Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

See agent-workspace/memory/decisions/004-up07-context-threshold-opus47.md\n15\tset -euo pipefail\n16\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n17\t\n18\t# === L-S48d-1 lint flag — S57 ratify per KI-S54-1 (alt-guard recognition) ===\n19\t# bash-hook-lint Check 7 flags this file. Lint refinement to recognize `if X && grep` and\n28\t# `|| echo NN` deferred per KI-S54-1.\n29\t\n30\tWIND_DOWN=\"${STOCKFORGE_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS:-${ORCH_WIND_DOWN_TOKENS:-180000}}\"\n31\tCLIFF=\"${STOCKFORGE_CLIFF_TOKENS:-${ORCH_CLIFF_TOKENS:-220000}}\"\n32\t\n33\t[ \"${STOCKFORGE_WATCHDOG_DISABLE:-${ORCH_WATCHDOG_DISABLE:-0}}\" = \"1\" ] && exit 0\n34\t\n35\tPAYLOAD=$(cat)\n36\tTRANSCRIPT=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\n37\tlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\n38\tprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).transcript_path||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n39\tHOOK_EVENT=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\n40\tlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\n41\tprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).hook_event_name||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n42\t\n43\tif [ -z \"${TRANSCRIPT:-}\" ] || [ ! -f \"$TRANSCRIPT\" ]; then\n44\t  exit 0\n45\tfi\n46\t\n47\t# Compute running context size from transcript JSONL usage fields; fallback to file size / 3.5.\n48\tTOTAL=$(node -e \"\n49\tconst fs=require('fs');\n50\tconst path=process.argv[1];\n51\tfunction pickUsage(o){return o&&(o.message&&o.message.usage)||o.usage||o.response&&o.response.usage||null;}\n52\ttry{\n53\t  const raw=fs.readFileSync(path,'utf8');\n54\t  const lines=raw.split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n55\t  let latest=null;\n56\t  for(const line of lines){\n57\t    try{const o=JSON.parse(line);const u=pickUsage(o);\n58\t      if(u&&(u.input_tokens||u.cache_read_input_tokens||u.cache_creation_input_tokens))latest=u;\n59\t    }catch{}\n60\t  }\n61\t  let ctx=0;\n62\t  if(latest)ctx=(latest.input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_creation_input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_read_input_tokens||0);\n63\t  if(ctx===0){const stat=fs.statSync(path);ctx=Math.floor(stat.size/3.5);}\n64\t  console.log(ctx);\n65\t}catch{console.log(0);}\" \"$TRANSCRIPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n66\t\n67\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n68\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n69\tprintf '%s\\n' \"$TOTAL\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\"\n70\t\n71\tHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n72\tprintf '[%s] watchdog tokens=%s wind_down=%s cliff=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" \"$WIND_DOWN\" \"$CLIFF\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n73\t\n74\tWIND_DOWN_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down\"\n75\tWIND_DOWN_FIRED=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down-fired\"\n76\tCLIFF_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.cliff-fired\"\n77\tFAILED_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-FAILED\"\n78\tPRE_BLOCK_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint\"\n79\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs\"\n80\t\n81\t# HH-H.4 (S48m): if auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh wrote a pre-block marker\n82\t# (stale checkpoint near threshold), skip the entire spawn path. Marker is\n83\t# cleared by the verifier hook once checkpoint freshness restores.\n84\tif [ -f \"$PRE_BLOCK_MARKER\" ]; then\n85\t  printf '[%s] watchdog: PRE-BLOCKED by auto-reboot-handoff-verify (stale checkpoint); skipping reboot spawn\\n' \\\n86\t    \"$(date -Iseconds)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n87\t  exit 0\n88\tfi\n89\t\n90\t# Auto-recovery on prior failed reboot: clear once-only markers so retry can fire.\n91\t# Without this, .cliff-fired / .wind-down-fired persist after a UIPI/SendKeys failure\n92\t# (Windows secure-desktop), silently blocking ALL future reboot retries.\n93\tif [ -f \"$FAILED_MARKER\" ]; then\n94\t  rm -f \"$WIND_DOWN_FIRED\" \"$CLIFF_MARK\" \"$FAILED_MARKER\"\n95\t  printf '[%s] watchdog: prior auto-reboot FAILED — cleared once-only markers to retry\\n' \\\n96\t    \"$(date -Iseconds)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n97\tfi\n98\t\n99\t# S35 D7 stale-marker auto-clear (2026-05-01): if a once-only marker is older than 1 hour\n100\t# AND the current session_id differs from the one that fired the marker, clear it so future\n101\t# cliff/wind-down can fire fresh in the new session. Surfaced from S34-extension where\n102\t# `.cliff-fired` from a prior session blocked re-fire after partial reboot left no\n103\t# .auto-reboot-FAILED marker (e.g., user manually /clear'd before failure was detected).\n104\tSESSION_ID=$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | node -e \"\n105\tlet s=''; process.stdin.on('data',c=>s+=c);\n106\tprocess.stdin.on('end',()=>{ try { console.log(JSON.parse(s).session_id||''); } catch { console.log(''); } });\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n107\tclear_stale_marker() {\n108\t  local marker=\"$1\"\n109\t  [ ! -f \"$marker\" ] && return 0\n110\t  # Marker mtime in epoch seconds vs now: clear if >3600s old.\n111\t  local marker_age\n112\t  marker_age=$(($(date +%s) - $(stat -c %Y \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)))\n113\t  if [ \"$marker_age\" -gt 3600 ]; then\n114\t    # If marker recorded a session_id different from current, OR marker pre-dates SESSION_ID file: clear.\n115\t    local recorded_session\n116\t    recorded_session=$(grep -oE 'session_id=[^ ]+' \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null | head -1 | cut -d= -f2 || true)\n117\t    if [ -z \"$recorded_session\" ] || [ \"$recorded_session\" != \"$SESSION_ID\" ]; -f \"$TRANSCRIPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compute running context size from transcript JSONL usage fields; fallback to file size / 3.5.\nTOTAL=$(node -e \"\nconst fs=require('fs');\nconst path=process.argv[1];\nfunction pickUsage(o){return o&&(o.message&&o.message.usage)||o.usage||o.response&&o.response.usage||null;}\ntry{\n  const raw=fs.readFileSync(path,'utf8');\n  const lines=raw.split('\\n').filter(Boolean);\n  let latest=null;\n  for(const line of lines){\n    try{const o=JSON.parse(line);const u=pickUsage(o);\n      if(u&&(u.input_tokens||u.cache_read_input_tokens||u.cache_creation_input_tokens))latest=u;\n    }catch{}\n  }\n  let ctx=0;\n  if(latest)ctx=(latest.input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_creation_input_tokens||0)+(latest.cache_read_input_tokens||0);\n  if(ctx===0){const stat=fs.statSync(path);ctx=Math.floor(stat.size/3.5);}\n  console.log(ctx);\n}catch{console.log(0);}\" \"$TRANSCRIPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nprintf '%s\\n' \"$TOTAL\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\"\n\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nprintf '[%s] watchdog tokens=%s wind_down=%s cliff=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$TOTAL\" \"$WIND_DOWN\" \"$CLIFF\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nWIND_DOWN_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down\"\nWIND_DOWN_FIRED=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.wind-down-fired\"\nCLIFF_MARK=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.cliff-fired\"\nFAILED_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-FAILED\"\nPRE_BLOCK_MARKER=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.auto-reboot-PRE-BLOCKED-stale-checkpoint\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/handoff-logs\"\n\n# HH-H.4 (S48m): if auto-reboot-handoff-verify.sh wrote a pre-block marker\n# (stale checkpoint near threshold), skip the entire spawn path. then\n    # If marker recorded a session_id different from current, OR marker pre-dates SESSION_ID file: clear.\n    local recorded_session\n    recorded_session=$(grep -oE 'session_id=[^ ]+' \"$marker\" 2>/dev/null | head -1 | cut -d= -f2 || true)\n    if [ -z \"$recorded_session\" ] || [ \"$recorded_session\" != \"$SESSION_ID\" ]; then\n      rm -f \"$marker\"\n      printf '[%s] watchdog: cleared stale marker %s (age=%ss recorded_session=%s current_session=%s)\\n' \\\n        \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"$marker\" \"$marker_age\" \"${recorded_session:-none}\" \"${SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    fi\n  fi\n}\nclear_stale_marker \"$CLIFF_MARK\"\nclear_stale_marker \"$WIND_DOWN_FIRED\"\nclear_stale_marker \"$WIND_DOWN_MARK\"\n\nif [ \"$TOTAL\" -ge \"$WIND_DOWN\" ] && [ \"$TOTAL\" -lt \"$CLIFF\" ]; then\n  # HH-H.5b atomic marker claim (set -o noclobber) — only ONE concurrent\n  # invocation wins the race; race-loser sees `>` fail and skips.\n  if ( set -o noclobber; printf 'wind_down_crossed_at=%s session_id=%s\\ntokens=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"${SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" \"$TOTAL\" > \"$WIND_DOWN_MARK\" ) 2>/dev/null; Flags two\n6\t# staleness modes:\n7\t#   (A) Active phase appearing in current-execution.md not present (or\n8\t#       wrong status) in project.md Phase Goals Tracker.\n9\t#   (B) project.md mtime older than newest ADR in decisions/ (project.md\n10\t#       must reflect every architectural decision per the file's own header).\n11\t#\n12\t# Per HH-C of 009-S48-harness-hardening-middle-phase.md.\n13\t# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + standard POSIX tools only.\n14\t# Soft-warn exit 0 always; never blocks Stop chain.\n15\t\n16\tset -uo pipefail\n17\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n18\t\n19\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n20\tMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n21\tPROJECT_MD=\"$MEMORY_DIR/project.md\"\n22\tCURRENT_EXEC=\"$MEMORY_DIR/current-execution.md\"\n23\tDECISIONS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/decisions\"\n24\tSESSION_ID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-default}\"\n25\tMARKER=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.project-md-staleness-fired-${SESSION_ID}\"\n26\tLOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n27\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n28\t\n29\t# Idempotent: bail if already fired this session.\n30\t[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n31\t\n32\t# Bail gracefully if either file missing.\n33\t[ ! -f \"$PROJECT_MD\" ] || [ ! -f \"$CURRENT_EXEC\" ] && { touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true; exit 0; }\n34\t\n35\t# Disable pipefail — legitimate grep-no-match returns 1 and would tip ERR trap.\n36\tset +o pipefail\n37\t\n38\tWARN_COUNT=0\n39\t\n40\t# ============================================================================\n41\t# Check A: phase status alignment\n42\t# ============================================================================\n43\t# Extract active phase number from current-execution.md \"**Phase**: N — ...\" line.\n44\tACTIVE_PHASE=\"$(grep -m1 -E '^\\*\\*Phase\\*\\*:[[:space:]]*[0-9]' \"$CURRENT_EXEC\" 2>/dev/null \\\n45\t                | sed -E 's/^\\*\\*Phase\\*\\*:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?).*/\\1/')\"\n46\t\n47\tif [ -n \"$ACTIVE_PHASE\" ]; then\n48\t  # Check that project.md Phase Goals Tracker has a row for this phase with status\n49\t  # matching reality. Phase Goals Tracker rows look like:\n50\t  #   | 2.5 — Harness Hardening (NEW) | 8 tracks ... | IN PROGRESS | 2026-05-05 (S48) | - |\n51\t  # We grep the row and inspect column 4 (status).\n52\t  PHASE_ROW=\"$(grep -E \"^\\|[[:space:]]*${ACTIVE_PHASE}[[:space:]]+—\" \"$PROJECT_MD\" 2>/dev/null | head -1)\"\n53\t\n54\t  if [ -z \"$PHASE_ROW\" ]; Flags two\n# staleness modes:\n#   (A) Active phase appearing in current-execution.md not present (or\n#       wrong status) in project.md Phase Goals Tracker.\n#   (B) project.md mtime older than newest ADR in decisions/ (project.md\n#       must reflect every architectural decision per the file's own header).\n#\n# Per HH-C of 009-S48-harness-hardening-middle-phase.md.\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + standard POSIX tools only.\n# Soft-warn exit 0 always; never blocks Stop chain.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nPROJECT_MD=\"$MEMORY_DIR/project.md\"\nCURRENT_EXEC=\"$MEMORY_DIR/current-execution.md\"\nDECISIONS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/decisions\"\nSESSION_ID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-default}\"\nMARKER=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.project-md-staleness-fired-${SESSION_ID}\"\nLOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n\n# Idempotent: bail if already fired this session.\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Bail gracefully if either file missing.\n[ ! -f \"$PROJECT_MD\" ] || [ ! -f \"$CURRENT_EXEC\" ] && { touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true; exit 0; }\n\n# Disable pipefail — legitimate grep-no-match returns 1 and would tip ERR trap.\nset +o pipefail\n\nWARN_COUNT=0\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Check A: phase status alignment\n# ============================================================================\n# Extract active phase number from current-execution.md \"**Phase**: N — ...\" line.\nACTIVE_PHASE=\"$(grep -m1 -E '^\\*\\*Phase\\*\\*:[[:space:]]*[0-9]' \"$CURRENT_EXEC\" 2>/dev/null \\\n                | sed -E 's/^\\*\\*Phase\\*\\*:[[:space:]]*([0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?).*/\\1/')\"\n\nif [ -n \"$ACTIVE_PHASE\" ]; then\n  # Check that project.md Phase Goals Tracker has a row for this phase with status\n  # matching reality. Phase Goals Tracker rows look like:\n  #   | 2.5 — Harness Hardening (NEW) | 8 tracks ... | IN PROGRESS | 2026-05-05 (S48) | - |\n  # We grep the row and inspect column 4 (status).\n  PHASE_ROW=\"$(grep -E \"^\\|[[:space:]]*${ACTIVE_PHASE}[[:space:]]+—\" \"$PROJECT_MD\" 2>/dev/null | head -1)\"\n\n  if [ -z \"$PHASE_ROW\" ]; then\n    echo \"[project-md-staleness-check] WARN: active Phase $ACTIVE_PHASE absent from project.md Phase Goals Tracker.\" >&2\n    echo \"[project-md-staleness-check] Action: add row to project.md table.\" >&2\n    WARN_COUNT=$(( WARN_COUNT + 1 ))\n  else\n    # Extract status column (3rd | -separated field after leading |).\n    PHASE_STATUS=\"$(echo \"$PHASE_ROW\" | awk -F'|' '{gsub(/^[[:space:]]+|[[:space:]]+$/,\"\",$4); print $4}')\"\n    case \"$PHASE_STATUS\" in\n      \"IN PROGRESS\"|\"DONE\"|\"PAUSED\"|\"NOT STARTED\"|\"BLOCKED\")\n        : # ok shape\n        ;;\n      *)\n        echo \"[project-md-staleness-check] WARN: Phase $ACTIVE_PHASE status='$PHASE_STATUS' not in {IN PROGRESS,DONE,PAUSED,NOT STARTED,BLOCKED}.\" >&2\n        WARN_COUNT=$(( WARN_COUNT + 1 ))\n        ;;\n    esac\n\n    # Cross-check: current-execution.md says active phase IN PROGRESS, project.md must agree.\n    # current-execution.md \"Track status\" section enumerates phases with ✅/🔵/⏸️.\n    if echo \"$PHASE_ROW\" | grep -q \"DONE\"; then\n      # If project.md says DONE but current-execution.md still calls it active → mismatch.\n      if grep -qE \"Phase ${ACTIVE_PHASE} (IN PROGRESS|🔵|in progress)\" \"$CURRENT_EXEC\" 2>/dev/null; then\n        echo \"[project-md-staleness-check] WARN: Phase $ACTIVE_PHASE = DONE in project.md but IN PROGRESS in current-execution.md.\" >&2\n        WARN_COUNT=$(( WARN_COUNT + 1 ))\n      fi\n    fi\n  fi\nfi\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Check B: project.md mtime vs newest ADR\n# ============================================================================\nif [ -d \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" ]; then\n  PROJECT_MTIME=\"$(find \"$PROJECT_MD\" -printf '%T@\\n' 2>/dev/null | head -1)\"\n  NEWEST_ADR_MTIME=\"$(find \"$DECISIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name 'D-*.md' -type f -printf '%T@\\t%f\\n' 2>/dev/null \\\n                      | sort -rn | head -1)\"\n  NEWEST_ADR_TS=\"$(echo \"$NEWEST_ADR_MTIME\" | cut -f1)\"\n  NEWEST_ADR_NAME=\"$(echo \"$NEWEST_ADR_MTIME\" | cut -f2)\"\n\n  if [ -n \"$PROJECT_MTIME\" ] && [ -n \"$NEWEST_ADR_TS\" ]; then\n    # Compare floats via awk — bash arithmetic doesn't handle floats.\n    OLDER=\"$(awk -v p=\"$PROJECT_MTIME\" -v a=\"$NEWEST_ADR_TS\" 'BEGIN{print (p < a) ? 1 : 0}')\"\n    if [ \"$OLDER\" = \"1\" ]; v2 will block.\n# Ported from orch v2.2.0 (verbatim).\n\nset -uo pipefail\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nLOG_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nmkdir -p \"$LOG_DIR\"\nLINT_LOG=\"$LOG_DIR/.tool-call-first-lint.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)\"\n\ncontains_narration_verb() {\n  grep -qEi \\\n    '(Dispatching|Will run|Will dispatch|About to|Now (running|dispatching)|Awaiting (sandwich|task|spec|code|systematic|verifier|reviewer|implementer))' \\\n    2>/dev/null\n}\n\nget_verb_hint() {\n  local text=\"$1\"\n  printf '%s' \"$text\" | grep -oEi \\\n    '(Dispatching|Will run|Will dispatch|About to|Now (running|dispatching)|Awaiting (sandwich|task|spec|code|systematic|verifier|reviewer|implementer))[^.!?\\n]*' \\\n    2>/dev/null | head -1 || true\n}\n\nextract_last_assistant_turn() {\n  local transcript_path=\"$1\"\n  tail -c 32768 \"$transcript_path\" 2>/dev/null | awk '\n  {\n    if (index($0, \"\\\"role\\\":\\\"assistant\\\"\") > 0) { last_line = $0 }\n  }\n  END {\n    if (last_line == \"\") { exit 1 }\n    if (index(last_line, \"\\\"type\\\":\\\"tool_use\\\"\") > 0) { print \"HAS_TOOL_USE=1\" } else { print \"HAS_TOOL_USE=0\" }\n    s = last_line\n    while (length(s) > 0) {\n      i = index(s, \"\\\"text\\\":\\\"\")\n      if (i == 0) break\n      s = substr(s, i + 8)\n      j = index(s, \"\\\"\")\n      if (j == 0) break\n      val = substr(s, 1, j - 1)\n      if (length(val) > 0) print val\n      s = substr(s, j + 1)\n    }\n  }\n  '\n}\n\nemit_warning() {\n  local session_id=\"$1\" verb_hint=\"$2\"\n  local msg=\"[TS=$TS] [session=$session_id] mode_a_suspected=true narration_only=true verb_hint=\\\"$verb_hint\\\"\"\n  echo \"$msg\" >> \"$LINT_LOG\"\n  echo \"[tool-call-first-lint] WARNING: Mode-A suspected — narration verb without tool_use block detected. $msg\" >&2\n}\n\nSTDIN_JSON=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nTRANSCRIPT_PATH=\"\"\nSESSION_ID=\"\"\n\nif [[ -n \"$STDIN_JSON\" ]]; then\n  TRANSCRIPT_PATH=\"$(printf '%s' \"$STDIN_JSON\" | grep -o '\"transcript_path\":\"[^\"]*\"' | sed 's/\"transcript_path\":\"//;s/\"//' | head -1 || true)\"\n  SESSION_ID=\"$(printf '%s' \"$STDIN_JSON\" | grep -o '\"session_id\":\"[^\"]*\"' | sed 's/\"session_id\":\"//;s/\"//' | head -1 || true)\"\nfi\n\nif [[ -z \"$TRANSCRIPT_PATH\" ]] || [[ ! -f \"$TRANSCRIPT_PATH\" ]]; then exit 0; fi\n\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nif [[ ! -f \"$EXEC_FILE\" ]]; then exit 0; fi\nif ! grep -qE '^\\*\\*autonomous_mode\\*\\*:\\s*true' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null; then exit 0; fi\n\nTURN_OUTPUT=\"\"\nif ! TURN_OUTPUT=\"$(extract_last_assistant_turn \"$TRANSCRIPT_PATH\")\"; then\n  echo \"[TS=$TS] [session=${SESSION_ID:-unknown}] parse_error=no_assistant_turn_found\" >> \"$LINT_LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nif [[ -z \"$TURN_OUTPUT\" ]]; then\n  echo \"[TS=$TS] [session=${SESSION_ID:-unknown}] parse_error=empty_content\" >> \"$LINT_LOG\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nHAS_TOOL_USE_LINE=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TURN_OUTPUT\" | head -1)\"\nTEXT_CONTENT=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$TURN_OUTPUT\" | tail -n +2)\"\n\nHAS_TOOL_USE=\"0\"\n[[ \"$HAS_TOOL_USE_LINE\" == \"HAS_TOOL_USE=1\" ]] && HAS_TOOL_USE=\"1\"\n\nNARRATION_VERB_HIT=\"\"\nif [[ -n \"$TEXT_CONTENT\" ]]; then\n  if printf '%s' \"$TEXT_CONTENT\" | contains_narration_verb; then\n    NARRATION_VERB_HIT=\"$(get_verb_hint \"$TEXT_CONTENT\")\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n[[ -z \"$NARRATION_VERB_HIT\" ]] && exit 0\n[[ \"$HAS_TOOL_USE\" == \"1\" ]] && exit 0\n\nemit_warning \"${SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" \"$NARRATION_VERB_HIT\"\nexit 0\n","numLines":103,"startLine":1,"totalLines":103}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0ad3cf68-7cdb-48fa-a6ac-3b5faee759ef","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Soft-warn on miss.\n7\t#\n8\t# Per HH-C of agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/009-S48-harness-hardening-middle-phase.md\n9\t# and decision-discipline.md Rule 4b (D-026): lesson-synthesis mandatory at session-end.\n10\t# This hook is the deterministic complement codifying the mistake-log half of that rule.\n11\t#\n12\t# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + standard POSIX tools only.\n13\t# Soft-warn exit 0 always; never blocks Stop chain.\n14\t\n15\tset -uo pipefail\n16\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n17\t\n18\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n19\tMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n20\tSESSIONS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/sessions\"\n21\tSESSION_ID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-default}\"\n22\tMARKER=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-end-checklist-fired-${SESSION_ID}\"\n23\tLOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\n24\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n25\t\n26\t# Idempotent: bail if already fired this session.\n27\t[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n28\t\n29\t# Bail if sessions dir missing.\n30\t[ ! -f \"$LATEST_SESSION\" ] && { touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true; exit 0; }\n38\t\n39\t# Only audit logs written within last 4h (otherwise this is a Stop in a session that\n40\t# never wrote a log — which is itself the violation, surfaced by the existence check below).\n41\tRECENT_LOGS=\"$(find \"$SESSIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -mmin -240 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n42\t[[ \"$RECENT_LOGS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || RECENT_LOGS=0\n43\t\n44\tif [ \"$RECENT_LOGS\" -eq 0 ]; then\n45\t  echo \"[session-end-checklist-linter] WARN: no session log written in last 4h.\" >&2\n46\t  echo \"[session-end-checklist-linter] Action: write agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md before Stop.\" >&2\n47\t  echo \"[$TS] session-end-checklist-linter: no_recent_log session=$SESSION_ID\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n48\t  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n49\t  exit 0\n50\tfi\n51\t\n52\t# Pattern check on latest session log.\n53\t# Pass conditions (any one suffices):\n54\t#   1. References mistake-log file directly\n55\t#   2. Cites a M-S<N>-<M> entry\n56\t#   3. Explicit \"no mistakes this session\" / \"0 mistakes\" / \"no new mistakes\" attestation\n57\tPATTERNS=\"mistake-log|M-S[0-9]+-[0-9]+|no mistakes this session|no new mistakes|0 mistakes|0 NEW mistakes|no mistake entries|nothing to mistake-log\"\n58\t\n59\tif grep -qiE \"$PATTERNS\" \"$LATEST_SESSION\" 2>/dev/null; then\n60\t  # Pass — lesson-synthesis discipline observed.\n61\t  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n62\t  exit 0\n63\tfi\n64\t\n65\t# Miss — soft-warn.\n66\tLATEST_BASENAME=\"$(basename \"$LATEST_SESSION\")\"\n67\techo \"[session-end-checklist-linter] WARN: $LATEST_BASENAME does not mention mistake-log update or 'no mistakes this session'.\" >&2\n68\techo \"[session-end-checklist-linter] Per decision-discipline.md Rule 4b: every session-end MUST either:\" >&2\n69\techo \"[session-end-checklist-linter]   (a) cite a NEW M-S<N>-<M> entry written to agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md, OR\" >&2\n70\techo \"[session-end-checklist-linter]   (b) explicitly state 'no mistakes this session' as attestation.\" >&2\n71\techo \"[$TS] session-end-checklist-linter: missing_attestation session=$SESSION_ID file=$LATEST_BASENAME\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n72\t\n73\ttouch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n74\texit 0\n75\t"}]},"uuid":"29d03daf-21d5-4225-9165-ed2127c7c9de","timestamp":"2026-05-05T15:13:35.273Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\session-end-checklist-linter.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# session-end-checklist-linter.sh — HH-C.1 Stop hook\n#\n# Purpose: enforce session-end ritual discipline by verifying the most recent\n# session log mentions a mistake-log update (entry M-S<N>-<M> OR explicit\n# \"no mistakes this session\" attestation). Soft-warn on miss.\n#\n# Per HH-C of agent-workspace/session-plans/pending/009-S48-harness-hardening-middle-phase.md\n# and decision-discipline.md Rule 4b (D-026): lesson-synthesis mandatory at session-end.\n# This hook is the deterministic complement codifying the mistake-log half of that rule.\n#\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + standard POSIX tools only.\n# Soft-warn exit 0 always; never blocks Stop chain.\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nSESSIONS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/sessions\"\nSESSION_ID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-default}\"\nMARKER=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-end-checklist-fired-${SESSION_ID}\"\nLOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-hooks.log\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')\"\n\n# Idempotent: bail if already fired this session.\n[ -f \"$MARKER\" ] && exit 0\n\n# Bail if sessions dir missing.\n[ ! -d \"$SESSIONS_DIR\" ] && { touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true; exit 0; }\n\n# Find most recent session log (mtime sorted; first hit).\nLATEST_SESSION=\"$(find \"$SESSIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -printf '%T@\\t%p\\n' 2>/dev/null \\\n                  | sort -rn | head -1 | cut -f2)\"\n\n# Bail if no session logs exist.\n[ -z \"$LATEST_SESSION\" ] || [ ! -f \"$LATEST_SESSION\" ] && { touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true; exit 0; }\n\n# Only audit logs written within last 4h (otherwise this is a Stop in a session that\n# never wrote a log — which is itself the violation, surfaced by the existence check below).\nRECENT_LOGS=\"$(find \"$SESSIONS_DIR\" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.md' -type f -mmin -240 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\"\n[[ \"$RECENT_LOGS\" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] || RECENT_LOGS=0\n\nif [ \"$RECENT_LOGS\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  echo \"[session-end-checklist-linter] WARN: no session log written in last 4h.\" >&2\n  echo \"[session-end-checklist-linter] Action: write agent-workspace/memory/sessions/YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md before Stop.\" >&2\n  echo \"[$TS] session-end-checklist-linter: no_recent_log session=$SESSION_ID\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Pattern check on latest session log.\n# Pass conditions (any one suffices):\n#   1. References mistake-log file directly\n#   2. Cites a M-S<N>-<M> entry\n#   3. Explicit \"no mistakes this session\" / \"0 mistakes\" / \"no new mistakes\" attestation\nPATTERNS=\"mistake-log|M-S[0-9]+-[0-9]+|no mistakes this session|no new mistakes|0 mistakes|0 NEW mistakes|no mistake entries|nothing to mistake-log\"\n\nif grep -qiE \"$PATTERNS\" \"$LATEST_SESSION\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  # Pass — lesson-synthesis discipline observed.\n  touch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Miss — soft-warn.\nLATEST_BASENAME=\"$(basename \"$LATEST_SESSION\")\"\necho \"[session-end-checklist-linter] WARN: $LATEST_BASENAME does not mention mistake-log update or 'no mistakes this session'.\" >&2\necho \"[session-end-checklist-linter] Per decision-discipline.md Rule 4b: every session-end MUST either:\" >&2\necho \"[session-end-checklist-linter]   (a) cite a NEW M-S<N>-<M> entry written to agent-workspace/memory/mistake-log.md, OR\" >&2\necho \"[session-end-checklist-linter]   (b) explicitly state 'no mistakes this session' as attestation.\" >&2\necho \"[$TS] session-end-checklist-linter: missing_attestation session=$SESSION_ID file=$LATEST_BASENAME\" >> \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\ntouch \"$MARKER\" 2>/dev/null || true\nexit 0\n","numLines":75,"startLine":1,"totalLines":75}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"aa4d4aca-7819-43fa-8615-d36984f5614d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\n15\tset -euo pipefail\n16\t\n17\tSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\n18\tHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../session-start-bootstrap.sh\"\n19\t[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n20\t\n21\tTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\n22\ttrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n23\t\n24\tmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\" \\\n25\t         \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\" \\\n26\t         \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints\"\n27\t\n28\t# Hook requires latest.md exists else early-exits before queued-grill scan (line ~30).\n29\ttouch \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\n30\t\n31\trun_hook() {\n32\t  local source_val=\"${1:-startup}\"\n33\t  local payload\n34\t  payload=$(printf '{\"source\":\"%s\"}' \"$source_val\")\n35\t  # Run hook; suppress stdout/stderr (hook emits JSON + diagnostic on stdout).\n36\t  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n37\t}\n38\t\n39\tLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.queued-grill-fired.log\"\n40\t\n41\t# --- TC1: ACTIVE_SESSION extracts LARGEST `## S<N>` → fire_when 'S52' matches ---\n42\trm -f \"$LOG\"\n43\tcat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n44\t# Current Execution\n45\t\n46\t**Phase**: 3.5\n47\t\n48\t## S52 — latest row\n49\t## S51 — older\n50\tEOF\n51\t\n52\tcat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-master.md\" <<EOF\n53\t# Queued Grill — Master Index\n54\t\n55\t### Q-TC1 — Test entry\n56\t\n57\t- **fire_when**: example trigger references S52 active\n58\t- **status**: open\n59\tEOF\n60\t\n61\trun_hook \"startup\"\n62\t\n63\tif [ ! {"parentUuid":"7075184f-5984-431e-b856-299318e0f63a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01F16J137ZrGtupMcV72cuwc","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015skvGRWzWp6hUkPySpEHiV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\session-end-checklist-linter-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for session-end-checklist-linter.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S59).\n#\n# Hook purpose (HH-C.1 Stop): verify most-recent session log mentions a mistake-log\n# update (entry M-S<N>-<M> OR explicit \"no mistakes this session\" attestation).\n# Soft-warn on miss. Idempotent per session (marker file).\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with sessions/ dir + various log fixtures;\n# invoke hook; assert .session-hooks.log content reflects pass/warn classification\n# AND marker file is created (idempotency).\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — sessions dir missing → silent (marker still touched)\n#   TC2 — no recent (<4h) session log → WARN no_recent_log\n#   TC3 — recent log mentions M-S<N>-<M> → silent (pass attestation)\n#   TC4 — recent log says \"no mistakes this session\" → silent (pass attestation)\n#   TC5 — recent log lacks attestation → WARN missing_attestation\n#   TC6 — idempotent: second invocation no-op (marker present)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../session-end-checklist-linter.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local session_id=\"${1:-test-session-tc}\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$session_id\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: sessions/ dir missing → silent (no log entry) ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"tc1-session\"\n# Hook should silently exit; no log entry expected.\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"tc1-session\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook should silently exit when sessions/ missing; log entry found\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Marker still touched per hook contract.\nif [ ! -f \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-end-checklist-fired-tc1-session\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: marker should be touched even on silent path\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: sessions/ missing → silent + marker touched\"\n\n# --- TC2: no recent log (mtime > 4h old) → WARN no_recent_log ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\"\necho \"old log content with M-S1-1 mention\" > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2020-01-01-session-1.md\"\n# Force mtime old (>240 minutes ago).\ntouch -d \"5 hours ago\" \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2020-01-01-session-1.md\"\nrun_hook \"tc2-session\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ] || ! grep -q \"no_recent_log\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected 'no_recent_log' WARN in session-hooks.log\"\n  echo \"Log content:\"; cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: no recent (<4h) log → WARN no_recent_log\"\n\n# --- TC3: recent log mentions M-S<N>-<M> → silent (pass) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-05-05-session-99.md\" <<EOF\n# Session 99\n\nSome work happened. M-S99-1 was the new mistake catalogued this session.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc3-session\"\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"missing_attestation\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log mentions M-S99-1 attestation; should NOT emit missing_attestation WARN\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: M-S<N>-<M> attestation → silent (pass)\"\n\n# --- TC4: \"no mistakes this session\" attestation → silent (pass) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-05-05-session-100.md\" <<EOF\n# Session 100\n\nRoutine work; no mistakes this session.\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc4-session\"\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"missing_attestation\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: log says 'no mistakes this session'; should NOT emit missing_attestation WARN\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: 'no mistakes this session' attestation → silent (pass)\"\n\n# --- TC5: recent log lacks attestation → WARN missing_attestation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-05-05-session-101.md\" <<EOF\n# Session 101\n\nWrote some code. Two staleness modes:\n#   (A) Active phase from current-execution absent (or wrong status) in project.md tracker\n#   (B) project.md mtime older than newest ADR by >2h\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with project.md + current-execution.md +\n# decisions/ fixtures; invoke hook; assert .session-hooks.log content reflects warns.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — both files missing → silent (graceful bail)\n#   TC2 — clean alignment (active phase exists with status IN PROGRESS) → silent\n#   TC3 — active phase absent from project.md → WARN\n#   TC4 — phase status DONE in project.md but IN PROGRESS in current-execution → WARN\n#   TC5 — project.md mtime > 2h older than newest ADR → WARN\n#   TC6 — project.md mtime < 2h older than newest ADR → silent (within tolerance)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../project-md-staleness-check.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local session_id=\"${1:-test-session-tc}\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"$session_id\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/decisions\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: both files missing → silent (graceful bail) ---\nclean_state\nrun_hook \"tc1-session\"\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"tc1-session\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: hook should silently exit when project.md/current-execution.md missing\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: missing files → silent\"\n\n# --- TC2: clean alignment → silent ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**Phase**: 3.5 — Harness Deepening\nEOF\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/project.md\" <<EOF\n# Project State\n\n| Phase | Tracks | Status | Started | Closed |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 3.5 — Harness Deepening | 8 tracks | IN PROGRESS | 2026-05-05 | - |\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc2-session\"\nif [ -f \"$LOG\" ] && grep -q \"tc2-session\" \"$LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: clean alignment should NOT emit warns\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: clean alignment → silent\"\n\n# --- TC3: active phase absent from project.md → WARN ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**Phase**: 9 — Future Phase\nEOF\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/project.md\" <<EOF\n# Project State\n\n| Phase | Tracks | Status | Started | Closed |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 3.5 — Harness Deepening | 8 tracks | IN PROGRESS | 2026-05-05 | - |\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc3-session\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ] || ! grep -q \"tc3-session\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: expected WARN log entry for missing phase\"\n  cat \"$LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n# Should mention phase=9\nif ! grep -q \"phase=9\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: log should mention phase=9 in warn entry\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: active phase absent from project.md → WARN\"\n\n# --- TC4: project.md says DONE but current-execution.md says IN PROGRESS → WARN ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**Phase**: 2 — Done Phase\n\nPhase 2 IN PROGRESS active here.\nEOF\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/project.md\" <<EOF\n# Project State\n\n| Phase | Tracks | Status | Started | Closed |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 2 — Done Phase | 5 tracks | DONE | 2026-04-01 | 2026-04-15 |\nEOF\nrun_hook \"tc4-session\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LOG\" ] || ! grep -q \"tc4-session\" \"$LOG\"; {"parentUuid":"17e23bb8-adae-4754-a173-a621036bd907","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_016JhtR8RgR7KVnfA71XhBPC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NYRm5n9SDQS8azS6Zg4Bfg","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\tool-call-first-lint-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for tool-call-first-lint.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S59).\n#\n# Hook purpose (Stop hook): detect Mode-A (narrate-without-tool-call) by parsing\n# last assistant turn from transcript JSONL. Mode-A = narration verb present\n# (Dispatching/Will run/About to/...) WITHOUT tool_use block in same turn.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with current-execution.md (autonomous_mode\n# flag) + transcript JSONL fixture; pipe stdin JSON; assert .tool-call-first-lint.log\n# content reflects expected classification.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no transcript_path → silent\n#   TC2 — autonomous_mode != true → silent (early bail)\n#   TC3 — last assistant turn has tool_use → silent (not Mode-A)\n#   TC4 — narration verb + tool_use present → silent (not Mode-A)\n#   TC5 — narration verb without tool_use → WARN (Mode-A suspected)\n#   TC6 — clean text without narration verb → silent\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../tool-call-first-lint.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLINT_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.tool-call-first-lint.log\"\n\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous() {\n  local mode_value=\"${1:-true}\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n  cat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**autonomous_mode**: $mode_value\nEOF\n}\n\nwrite_transcript() {\n  local fixture_content=\"$1\"\n  local fixture_path=\"$TEMPDIR/transcript.jsonl\"\n  printf '%s\\n' \"$fixture_content\" > \"$fixture_path\"\n  echo \"$fixture_path\"\n}\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local transcript_path=\"$1\"\n  local session_id=\"${2:-test-session-tc}\"\n  local payload\n  payload=$(printf '{\"session_id\":\"%s\",\"transcript_path\":\"%s\"}' \"$session_id\" \"$transcript_path\")\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/transcript.jsonl\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no transcript_path → silent ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\n# Empty transcript_path in payload.\nPAYLOAD='{\"session_id\":\"tc1\",\"transcript_path\":\"\"}'\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\nif [ -f \"$LINT_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"tc1\" \"$LINT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null;
