S59).\n#\n# Hook purpose (Stop hook): detect Mode-A (narrate-without-tool-call) by parsing\n# last assistant turn from transcript JSONL. Mode-A = narration verb present\n# (Dispatching/Will run/About to/...) WITHOUT tool_use block in same turn.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with current-execution.md (autonomous_mode\n# flag) + transcript JSONL fixture; pipe stdin JSON; assert .tool-call-first-lint.log\n# content reflects expected classification.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — no transcript_path → silent\n#   TC2 — autonomous_mode != true → silent (early bail)\n#   TC3 — last assistant turn has tool_use → silent (not Mode-A)\n#   TC4 — narration verb + tool_use present → silent (not Mode-A)\n#   TC5 — narration verb without tool_use → WARN (Mode-A suspected)\n#   TC6 — clean text without narration verb → silent\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../tool-call-first-lint.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nLINT_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.tool-call-first-lint.log\"\n\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous() {\n  local mode_value=\"${1:-true}\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n  cat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**autonomous_mode**: $mode_value\nEOF\n}\n\nwrite_transcript() {\n  local fixture_content=\"$1\"\n  local fixture_path=\"$TEMPDIR/transcript.jsonl\"\n  printf '%s\\n' \"$fixture_content\" > \"$fixture_path\"\n  echo \"$fixture_path\"\n}\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local transcript_path=\"$1\"\n  local session_id=\"${2:-test-session-tc}\"\n  local payload\n  payload=$(printf '{\"session_id\":\"%s\",\"transcript_path\":\"%s\"}' \"$session_id\" \"$transcript_path\")\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/transcript.jsonl\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: no transcript_path → silent ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\n# Empty transcript_path in payload.\nPAYLOAD='{\"session_id\":\"tc1\",\"transcript_path\":\"\"}'\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD\" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\nif [ -f \"$LINT_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"tc1\" \"$LINT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: empty transcript_path should silently exit (no log entry)\"\n  cat \"$LINT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: empty transcript_path → silent\"\n\n# --- TC2: autonomous_mode != true → silent (early bail before transcript parse) ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"false\"\nTRANSCRIPT_FILE=$(write_transcript '{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Dispatching the verifier\"}]}')\nrun_hook \"$TRANSCRIPT_FILE\" \"tc2-session\"\nif [ -f \"$LINT_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"tc2-session\" \"$LINT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: autonomous_mode=false should bail before classification\"\n  cat \"$LINT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: autonomous_mode != true → silent (early bail)\"\n\n# --- TC3: last assistant turn HAS tool_use → silent ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\nTRANSCRIPT_FILE=$(write_transcript '{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Reading the file\"},{\"type\":\"tool_use\",\"id\":\"toolu_01\",\"name\":\"Read\",\"input\":{\"file_path\":\"x.md\"}}]}')\nrun_hook \"$TRANSCRIPT_FILE\" \"tc3-session\"\n# Hook should classify HAS_TOOL_USE=1 → silent (no warn entry).\nif [ -f \"$LINT_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true.*tc3-session\" \"$LINT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: tool_use present → should NOT classify as Mode-A\"\n  cat \"$LINT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: tool_use present → silent\"\n\n# --- TC4: narration verb + tool_use both present → silent (not Mode-A) ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\nTRANSCRIPT_FILE=$(write_transcript '{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Dispatching the sandwich-verifier now\"},{\"type\":\"tool_use\",\"id\":\"toolu_02\",\"name\":\"Agent\",\"input\":{\"prompt\":\"verify\"}}]}')\nrun_hook \"$TRANSCRIPT_FILE\" \"tc4-session\"\nif [ -f \"$LINT_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true.*tc4-session\" \"$LINT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: tool_use accompanies narration verb → should NOT classify as Mode-A\"\n  cat \"$LINT_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: narration verb + tool_use → silent\"\n\n# --- TC5: narration verb WITHOUT tool_use → WARN (Mode-A suspected) ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\nTRANSCRIPT_FILE=$(write_transcript '{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Dispatching the verifier shortly\"}]}')\nrun_hook \"$TRANSCRIPT_FILE\" \"tc5-session\"\nif [ ! -f \"$LINT_LOG\" ] || ! grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true.*tc5-session\" \"$LINT_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected mode_a_suspected=true WARN entry\"\n  cat \"$LINT_LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: narration verb without tool_use → WARN Mode-A suspected\"\n\n# --- TC6: clean text (no narration verb, no tool_use) → silent ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\nTRANSCRIPT_FILE=$(write_transcript '{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Result summary: completed three tasks.\"}]}')\nrun_hook \"$TRANSCRIPT_FILE\" \"tc6-session\"\nif [ -f \"$LINT_LOG\" ] && grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true.*tc6-session\" \"$LINT_LOG\" 2>/dev/null; Soft-warn unless STOCKFORGE_CITATION_STRICT=1.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp files in audit dirs, pipe stdin JSON simulating\n# PostToolUse payload, assert .citation-violations.log + .session-hooks.log\n# content reflects expected per-case classification.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — tool_name=Read (not Write/Edit) → silent (early bail)\n#   TC2 — file_path outside audit dirs → silent (early bail)\n#   TC3 — file in audit dir, no numeric claims → silent\n#   TC4 — numeric claim WITH source/as_of nearby → silent\n#   TC5 — numeric claim WITHOUT source/as_of → WARN (citation-missing)\n#   TC6 — STOCKFORGE_CITATION_STRICT=1 + violation → emit block JSON to stdout\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../post-tool-citation-grep.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nVIOL_LOG=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.citation-violations.log\"\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local tool_name=\"$1\" file_path=\"$2\" strict=\"${3:-0}\"\n  local payload\n  payload=$(printf '{\"tool_name\":\"%s\",\"tool_input\":{\"file_path\":\"%s\"}}' \"$tool_name\" \"$file_path\")\n  STOCKFORGE_CITATION_STRICT=\"$strict\" CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" 2>/dev/null\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n}\n\n# --- TC1: tool_name=Read → silent (early bail before file check) ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\necho \"ROE 18%\" > \"$TEMPDIR/specs/foo.md\"\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook \"Read\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs/foo.md\")\nif [ -n \"$STDOUT\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: tool_name=Read should produce no stdout\"; echo \"Got: $STDOUT\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$VIOL_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: tool_name=Read should not create violations log\"\n  cat \"$VIOL_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: tool_name=Read → silent (early bail)\"\n\n# --- TC2: file outside audit dirs → silent ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/random-dir\"\necho \"ROE 18%\" > \"$TEMPDIR/random-dir/foo.md\"\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook \"Edit\" \"$TEMPDIR/random-dir/foo.md\")\nif [ -n \"$STDOUT\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: file outside audit dirs should produce no stdout\"; echo \"Got: $STDOUT\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$VIOL_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: file outside audit dirs should not create violations log\"\n  cat \"$VIOL_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: file outside audit dirs → silent\"\n\n# --- TC3: audit dir, no numeric claims → silent ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/specs/clean.md\" <<EOF\n# Clean spec\n\nThis spec has prose only. No numeric values worth citing.\nEOF\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook \"Edit\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs/clean.md\")\nif [ -n \"$STDOUT\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: clean file should produce no stdout\"; echo \"Got: $STDOUT\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$VIOL_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC3: clean file should not log violations\"\n  cat \"$VIOL_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC3: no numeric claims → silent\"\n\n# --- TC4: numeric claim WITH source/as_of nearby → silent ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/specs\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/specs/cited.md\" <<EOF\n# Cited spec\n\nROE 18% (above industry average)\nsource: vnstock fundamentals API\nas_of: 2026-05-05\nEOF\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook \"Edit\" \"$TEMPDIR/specs/cited.md\")\nif [ -n \"$STDOUT\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: cited file should produce no stdout\"; echo \"Got: $STDOUT\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$VIOL_LOG\" ] && [ -s \"$VIOL_LOG\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: cited file should not log violations\"\n  cat \"$VIOL_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: numeric claim with source/as_of nearby → silent\"\n\n# --- TC5: numeric claim WITHOUT source/as_of → log violation ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/uncited.md\" <<EOF\n# Uncited thesis\n\nSome thesis claims:\n- ROE 18%\n- Revenue growth 35% YoY\n- Market cap 12000 billion VND\n\nNo provenance metadata anywhere here.\nEOF\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook \"Edit\" \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/uncited.md\")\n# Soft-warn: stdout may be empty (warn goes to stderr); violations should be logged.\nif [ ! -f \"$VIOL_LOG\" ] || ! grep -q \"CITATION-MISSING\" \"$VIOL_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected CITATION-MISSING entries in $VIOL_LOG\"\n  cat \"$VIOL_LOG\" 2>&1 || echo \"(no log)\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: numeric claim without source/as_of → WARN (violation logged)\"\n\n# --- TC6: STRICT mode + violation → emit block JSON to stdout ---\nclean_state\nmkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log\"\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/strict-violation.md\" <<EOF\n# Strict-mode test\n\nBare claims again: ROE 22%, revenue 15000 billion VND, market cap 8500 tỷ.\nEOF\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook \"Edit\" \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/thesis-log/strict-violation.md\" \"1\")\nif [ -z \"$STDOUT\" ] || ! printf '%s' \"$STDOUT\" | grep -q '\"decision\":\"block\"'; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: STRICT mode + violation should emit block JSON to stdout\"\n  echo \"Got stdout: '$STDOUT'\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! printf '%s' \"$STDOUT\" | grep -q \"STOCKFORGE I-S2\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: block JSON should reference 'STOCKFORGE I-S2'\"\n  echo \"Got stdout: $STDOUT\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: STRICT mode + violation → block JSON emitted\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"post-tool-citation-grep.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":320,"cache_read_input_tokens":145036,"output_tokens":3392,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":320,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3392,"cache_read_input_tokens":145036,"cache_creation_input_tokens":320,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":320},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CajeTcuxPprUgDDCLJSiu","type":"assistant","uuid":"9c629805-8946-4bac-b003-6fe16479d4ec","timestamp":"2026-05-05T15:19:06.453Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"6f6042ae-883c-40f0-bdcb-c0331baf81c3","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01Gx1Uknehf8ssHu9UD7vxF7","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XgjSZQfJcpsxRzYAogsc6x","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\firing-tests\\budget-watchdog-fire-test.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Firing-test for budget-watchdog.sh (Phase 3.5 T7 retrofit; S59).\n#\n# Hook purpose (PostToolUse + Stop): read transcript JSONL, compute real token\n# usage via node, fire wind-down/cliff auto-reboot at thresholds. Mode-C guard\n# (Stop only) blocks premature turn-end when LLM cites budget pressure but real\n# tokens well under threshold.\n#\n# Test strategy: stage temp PROJECT_DIR with stubbed session-self-reboot.sh +\n# current-execution.md fixture + transcript JSONL containing `usage` records;\n# pipe stdin JSON simulating PostToolUse / Stop payload; assert observable\n# state changes (.transcript-tokens, marker files, stdout decision JSON,\n# .session-hooks.log entries).\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — STOCKFORGE_WATCHDOG_DISABLE=1 → silent (no .transcript-tokens written)\n#   TC2 — empty transcript_path → silent\n#   TC3 — normal token compute → .transcript-tokens written + log entry\n#   TC4 — TOTAL ≥ CLIFF → .cliff-fired marker created (atomic noclobber claim)\n#   TC5 — Mode-C guard fires (Stop + autonomous_mode=true + TOTAL<SOFT_LIMIT\n#         + no .wind-down marker + transcript tail has premature-windown verb)\n#         → emit decision block JSON to stdout\n#   TC6 — Mode-C guard does NOT fire when TOTAL ≥ SOFT_LIMIT (verb match alone\n#         is insufficient if real tokens are above threshold)\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../budget-watchdog.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\n# Need node (hook depends on it for JSONL parse). Bail-skip if absent.\nif ! command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  echo \"SKIP: node not available — budget-watchdog hook depends on node for transcript parse\"\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\nstub_reboot_script() {\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/scripts\"\n  cat > \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/session-self-reboot.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Stub for firing-test — record invocation, exit success.\necho \"STUB-REBOOT-INVOKED args=$*\" >&2\nexit 0\nEOF\n  chmod +x \"$TEMPDIR/scripts/session-self-reboot.sh\"\n}\n\nwrite_transcript() {\n  local input_tokens=\"$1\"\n  local extra_content=\"${2:-}\"\n  local fixture_path=\"$TEMPDIR/transcript.jsonl\"\n  cat > \"$fixture_path\" <<EOF\n{\"role\":\"system\",\"content\":\"setup\"}\n{\"role\":\"assistant\",\"message\":{\"usage\":{\"input_tokens\":$input_tokens,\"cache_creation_input_tokens\":0,\"cache_read_input_tokens\":0}}}\nEOF\n  if [ -n \"$extra_content\" ]; then\n    printf '%s\\n' \"$extra_content\" >> \"$fixture_path\"\n  fi\n  echo \"$fixture_path\"\n}\n\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous() {\n  local mode_value=\"${1:-true}\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n  cat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\" <<EOF\n# Current Execution\n\n**autonomous_mode**: $mode_value\nEOF\n}\n\nrun_hook_payload() {\n  local payload=\"$1\"\n  local disable=\"${2:-0}\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" STOCKFORGE_WATCHDOG_DISABLE=\"$disable\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" 2>/dev/null\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\" \"$TEMPDIR/transcript.jsonl\" \"$TEMPDIR/scripts\"\n  mkdir -p \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n  stub_reboot_script\n}\n\n# --- TC1: STOCKFORGE_WATCHDOG_DISABLE=1 → silent ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\nTRANSCRIPT_FILE=$(write_transcript \"100000\")\nPAYLOAD=$(printf '{\"session_id\":\"tc1\",\"transcript_path\":\"%s\",\"hook_event_name\":\"PostToolUse\"}' \"$TRANSCRIPT_FILE\")\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook_payload \"$PAYLOAD\" \"1\")\nif [ -n \"$STDOUT\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: WATCHDOG_DISABLE=1 should produce no stdout\"; echo \"Got: $STDOUT\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif [ -f \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: WATCHDOG_DISABLE=1 should not write .transcript-tokens\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: WATCHDOG_DISABLE=1 → silent (no state mutation)\"\n\n# --- TC2: empty transcript_path → silent (early bail before token compute) ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\nPAYLOAD='{\"session_id\":\"tc2\",\"transcript_path\":\"\",\"hook_event_name\":\"PostToolUse\"}'\nSTDOUT=$(run_hook_payload \"$PAYLOAD\")\nif [ -f \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: empty transcript_path should not write .transcript-tokens\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: empty transcript_path → silent (early bail)\"\n\n# --- TC3: normal compute → .transcript-tokens + log entry ---\nclean_state\nwrite_exec_with_autonomous \"true\"\nTRANSCRIPT_FILE=$(write_transcript \"75000\")\nPAYLOAD=$(printf '{\"session_id\":\"tc3\",\"transcript_path\":\"%s\",\"hook_event_name\":\"PostToolUse\"}' \"$TRANSCRIPT_FILE\")\nrun_hook_payload \"$PAYLOAD\" >/dev/null\nTOKENS_FILE=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/.transcript-tokens\"\nif [ ! -f \"$TOKENS_FILE\" ]; {"parentUuid":"2303ce9c-0f31-4884-9c51-1ae3367d4b81","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"7f5d15b0-0285-49c9-836a-9fed59534dfa","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EJUcsMJT6id8CsoFEZNuvT","type":"tool_result","content":"- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/session-start-bootstrap-fire-test.sh` TC5 + TC6 demonstrate recipe validation pattern\r\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S57-KI-S56-1-fix-and-L-S48d-1-sample.log` § Deliverable #1\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### L-S58-1 — Lint heuristic refinement for ERR-trap-aware bare-grep detection (KI-S54-1 closure)\r\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\r\n**Context**: KI-S54-1 fix at S58 — replaced bash-hook-lint Check 7 narrow alt-guard whitelist (`|| true` / `|| :` ONLY) with broad-spectrum awk-based heuristic that exercises 4 capabilities derived from rigorous reading of bash(1) ERR-trap exemption rules + pipefail semantics.\r\n\r\n**Rule (CONCRETE recipe for ERR-trap-aware static analysis of bash hooks)**:\r\n\r\n1. **State-aware processing** (use awk, not piped grep filters) — any rule that requires regional context (e.g. \"is this grep inside a pipefail-disabled bracket?\") needs line-by-line state tracking. Awk's pattern-action model + variables enables this in pure POSIX. Skip rule: maintain `pipefail_off` flag toggled by `set +o pipefail` (off) and `set -[a-zA-Z]*o pipefail` (on); skip greps when `pipefail_off=1`. Initial state: OFF (matches bash default; flips ON when first `set -o pipefail` seen).\r\n\r\n2. **Multi-line `\\`-continuation joining** — physical lines ending with `\\` accumulate; pattern testing runs on joined logical line. Catches alt-guards (`|| echo 0`) placed on the LAST continuation line of an N-line pipeline. Without joining, the FIRST continuation line containing grep would be flagged even when the pipeline is properly guarded.\r\n\r\n3. **Broad `&&`/`||` chain rule subsumes narrow whitelist** — bash(1) spec: \"any command executed in a && or || list except the command following the final && or ||\" is ERR-trap exempt. So `grep` followed by ANY `&&` or `||` operator on the same line means grep is non-final, exempt. Detection regex: `grep[[:space:]].*[[:space:]](&&|\\|\\|)[[:space:]]`. Subsumes the narrow whitelist (`|| true|:|echo|exit|return`) while ALSO covering arbitrary chain commands like `[ X ] && grep -qiE pat && DOCUMENTED=1` (ghost-work-audit:39 form). Crucially, the regex requires `[[:space:]]` AROUND the operator → distinguishes shell `||` operator from regex alternation `|` inside quoted patterns (e.g. `grep '(true|false)'` correctly NOT skipped — single `|`, no space-`|`-space).\r\n\r\n4. **Compound-conditional skip via line-start anchor** — line starts with `if|while|until|elif` AND contains `grep` → skip. Catches `if [ X ] && grep ...; then` and `if X | grep ...; then` forms. Per bash(1) spec: command in `if`/`while`/`until` test list is exempt. Note: `for` is NOT in the exemption list — `for ref in $(grep ...)` IS unsafe with pipefail+ERR-trap (cmd-sub exit propagates).\r\n\r\n**Anti-pattern cases this rule correctly catches** (real bare-greps requiring fix):\r\n- `RESULT=$(grep ... | head -1)` — pipefail makes pipeline exit non-zero on grep no-match → cmd-sub exits 1 → `set -e` triggers ERR trap.\r\n- `for ref in $(grep ... | sort -u); do` — same; `for` is NOT exempt.\r\n- `grep ... | other` — pipeline exit propagates through pipefail.\r\n- `[ X ] && grep ...` (grep as final command) — per spec, command FOLLOWING final `&&` is NOT exempt.\r\n\r\n**Anti-pattern cases this rule correctly skips** (semantically safe):\r\n- `if grep ...; then` (direct conditional)\r\n- `while grep ...; do` (loop conditional)\r\n- `if [ X ] && grep ...; then` (compound conditional)\r\n- `if cmd | grep ...; then` (pipeline conditional)\r\n- `grep ... && action` (`&&` chain non-final)\r\n- `grep ... || true` (alt-guard true)\r\n- `grep ... || echo NN` (alt-guard echo)\r\n- `grep ... || exit 0` (alt-guard exit)\r\n- `set +o pipefail; grep; set -o pipefail` (pipefail-bracket double-defense)\r\n- `[ X ] && grep ... && cmd` (`&&` chain mid-position)\r\n\r\n**Process substitution `<(grep ...)`**: subshell exit doesn't propagate to parent's ERR trap → semantically safe per bash spec. But adding `|| true` inside is canonical + makes lint happy. Recommendation: apply `|| true` for documentation + lint compliance even when ERR-trap-safe.\r\n\r\n**Validation**: 17/17 firing-test cases PASS (11 baseline + 6 NEW S58 covering all 4 capabilities). Production smoke validated empirically: L-S48d-1 violations 27 → 0 across `scripts/hooks/` after Check 7 awk refinement + 11 surgical `|| true` fixes in 6 hooks (checkpoint-marker-cleanup-resume + checkpoint-write-marker + correction-rate-tracker + drift-rollup-daily + ghost-work-audit + stale-prompt-detector).\r\n\r\n**Severity**: medium — generalizable for any future static analysis where bash-spec ERR-trap exemption rules govern correctness. Reduces lint false-positive rate from ~74% (20 of 27 flags pre-S58 were S57-categorized safe forms) to <5% (the residual is pre-existing L-S11-1 + L-S53-2 + 1 D-IDENTITY false-positive, unrelated to L-S48d-1).\r\n\r\n**Auto-detect**: full — Check 7 awk implementation IS the deterministic detection; firing-test discipline (L-S51-1) ensures regression coverage; production smoke validates empirically.\r\n\r\n**Where tracked**:\r\n- This entry (L-S58-1)\r\n- `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 7 block (S58 awk implementation; ~54 lines incl. comment block)\r\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/bash-hook-lint-fire-test.sh` TCs (TC-C-tricky-bare + TC-C-compound-and + TC-C-compound-pipe + TC-C-alt-echo + TC-C-bracket + TC-C-and-chain)\r\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S58-bash-hook-lint-Check7-broad-refinement-and-L-S48d-1-cleanup.log` (full session quality report)","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9d8b71dc-4192-4f5c-ac41-7665ad40a796","timestamp":"2026-05-05T15:24:49.214Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/session-start-bootstrap-fire-test.sh` TC5 + TC6 demonstrate recipe validation pattern\r\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S57-KI-S56-1-fix-and-L-S48d-1-sample.log` § Deliverable #1\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n### L-S58-1 — Lint heuristic refinement for ERR-trap-aware bare-grep detection (KI-S54-1 closure)\r\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\r\n**Context**: KI-S54-1 fix at S58 — replaced bash-hook-lint Check 7 narrow alt-guard whitelist (`|| true` / `|| :` ONLY) with broad-spectrum awk-based heuristic that exercises 4 capabilities derived from rigorous reading of bash(1) ERR-trap exemption rules + pipefail semantics.\r\n\r\n**Rule (CONCRETE recipe for ERR-trap-aware static analysis of bash hooks)**:\r\n\r\n1. **State-aware processing** (use awk, not piped grep filters) — any rule that requires regional context (e.g. \"is this grep inside a pipefail-disabled bracket?\") needs line-by-line state tracking. {"parentUuid":"20b3cda2-07e6-4078-8cd3-b770d8f73687","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01VZ2q4dFjeaZjWrsGtdgD3V","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011mdHFyGEY5s6Qy4mgnqQS6","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","old_string":"**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S58-1)\n- `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 7 block (S58 awk implementation; ~54 lines incl. comment block)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/bash-hook-lint-fire-test.sh` TCs (TC-C-tricky-bare + TC-C-compound-and + TC-C-compound-pipe + TC-C-alt-echo + TC-C-bracket + TC-C-and-chain)\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S58-bash-hook-lint-Check7-broad-refinement-and-L-S48d-1-cleanup.log` (full session quality report)","new_string":"**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S58-1)\n- `scripts/hooks/bash-hook-lint.sh` Check 7 block (S58 awk implementation; ~54 lines incl. comment block)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/bash-hook-lint-fire-test.sh` TCs (TC-C-tricky-bare + TC-C-compound-and + TC-C-compound-pipe + TC-C-alt-echo + TC-C-bracket + TC-C-and-chain)\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S58-bash-hook-lint-Check7-broad-refinement-and-L-S48d-1-cleanup.log` (full session quality report)\n\n---\n\n### L-S59-1 — T7 retrofit firing-tests target externally observable behavior, NOT internal implementation\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Context**: S59 batch retrofit of 5 hooks (session-end-checklist-linter, project-md-staleness-check, tool-call-first-lint, post-tool-citation-grep, budget-watchdog). 30 NEW TCs all PASS first-iteration (after one regex iteration-bug catch per L-S52-3).\n\n**Rule**: T7 retrofit firing-tests should validate **externally observable behavior** — the hook's contract with the harness — NOT internal implementation details. Externally observable surface = (a) log file writes (`.session-hooks.log` / `.tool-call-first-lint.log` / `.citation-violations.log` / etc.); (b) marker file presence/content (`.session-end-checklist-fired-*` / `.cliff-fired` / `.wind-down-fired` / `.transcript-tokens` / etc.); (c) stdout decision JSON (`{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":...}` for Mode-C guard / STRICT-mode citation block); (d) hook stderr WARN messages.\n\nInternal implementation = specific grep patterns, sed transformations, awk extraction logic, branch ordering. These over-specify and break on legitimate refactors.\n\n**Why**: \n- Tests validating *internal* logic (e.g. \"this exact awk pattern matches\") break when fix recipes legitimately refactor internals (witness S57 KI-S56-1 awk-range refactor needing TC4 fixture update — that test was over-specifying internals).\n- Tests validating *externally observable behavior* survive refactors as long as the hook's contract holds. They're the regression-coverage source of truth for the harness.\n- Hook contracts with harness ARE the externally observable surface; that's exactly what a fresh-context drift scan checks per HH-A..HH-H discipline.\n\n**How to apply**:\n- For each TC, describe a stimulus (env var like `WATCHDOG_DISABLE=1` / `STOCKFORGE_CITATION_STRICT=1`; payload like `{\"tool_name\":\"Read\",...}`; fixture file like JSONL transcript / current-execution.md / mistake-log mention).\n- Assert expected externally-observable outcome (log line presence / marker file presence + content / stdout JSON shape / stderr WARN count).\n- DO NOT assert internal grep regex matched OR specific awk variables set. If internals change but contract holds, test should pass.\n- Use helper functions for repeated patterns (e.g. `session_classified_mode_a()` extracting two-substring lookup; `write_transcript()` constructing JSONL fixture with given input_tokens). Helpers reduce assertion-regex iteration-bugs (per L-S59-2).\n\n**Recipe (concrete)**:\n```bash\n# Per-TC pattern:\nclean_state                          # reset temp PROJECT_DIR\nwrite_fixture \"...\"                  # stage stimulus inputs\nrun_hook \"$payload\" \"$envvar\"        # invoke hook with controlled inputs\nassert_observable \"$expected_log\" || { echo \"FAIL TCN: ...\"; exit 1; }\necho \"PASS TCN: ...\"\n```\n\n**Validation**: 5 hooks × 6 TCs = 30/30 PASS first-iteration (after 1 assertion-regex iteration-bug catch per L-S52-3). 0 hook source changes required (categorization confirmed all 5 hooks lint-clean post-S58 broad chain rule). Cumulative T7 firing-tests: 78/78 PASS across 10 hooks.\n\n**Severity**: medium — codifies retrofit recipe for remaining ~16 untested hooks. Generalizable: applies to any deterministic hook whose contract is \"stimulus → state mutation / decision\".\n\n**Auto-detect**: partial — hook-firing-counter surfaces silent-≥7d hooks as priority queue; firing-test ABSENCE for active hooks remains a gap (T6 self-scan future work per Phase 3.5 plan 010).\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S59-1)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/{session-end-checklist-linter,project-md-staleness-check,tool-call-first-lint,post-tool-citation-grep,budget-watchdog}-fire-test.sh` (5 NEW; 6 TCs each)\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log`\n\n---\n\n### L-S59-2 — Two-stage grep beats single-regex `.*` chaining for multi-substring log assertions\n**Date**: 2026-05-05\n**Context**: S59 firing-test for tool-call-first-lint hit pre-deploy iteration-bug at TC5 — assertion `grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true.*tc5-session\"` failed because real log line is `[TS=...] [session=tc5-session] mode_a_suspected=true ...` (session ID precedes classification keyword).\n\n**Rule**: When asserting that a log line contains MULTIPLE substrings, use **two-stage grep**:\n```bash\ngrep \"anchor=$VAL\" \"$LOG\" | grep -q \"$keyword\"\n```\nNOT single-regex `.*` chaining:\n```bash\ngrep -q \"$keyword.*anchor=$VAL\" \"$LOG\"  # Order-dependent; brittle\n```\n\n**Why**:\n- Two-stage grep is order-independent: works regardless of which substring appears first.\n- Single-regex `.*` chaining requires the writer of the assertion to know the EXACT order in which the LOG-WRITER emitted substrings. The two are usually different humans (or the same human at different times), and ordering drift is undetected at code-review time.\n- Generalizable to ANY log-line assertion: emit_warning() functions in different hooks order substrings differently; assertion-writer should not be coupled.\n\n**How to apply**:\n- Extract a helper function for repeated assertion patterns: e.g. `session_classified_mode_a() { grep \"session=$1\" \"$LOG\" | grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true\"; }`.\n- For one-off assertions, write two-stage grep directly.\n\n**Validation**: TC5 of tool-call-first-lint-fire-test.sh — pre-deploy single-regex assertion FAILED on real log line; refactored to `session_classified_mode_a()` helper applied uniformly to TC3/TC4/TC5/TC6 → all 4 TCs PASS. Caught at firing-test stage = SUCCESS path of L-S51-1 per L-S52-3 doctrine; NOT catalogued as M-S59-X.\n\n**Severity**: low — narrowly applicable to log-line assertion authoring. But codifying prevents the same iteration-bug class in the next ~16 T7 retrofit firing-tests.\n\n**Auto-detect**: none — discovered empirically on first wrong assertion regex; no static-lint detection candidate (regex-vs-actual-output is per-hook semantics).\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S59-2)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/tool-call-first-lint-fire-test.sh` `session_classified_mode_a()` helper (line ~58-64)\n- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bug catches"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":401,"cache_read_input_tokens":171318,"output_tokens":3195,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":401,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3195,"cache_read_input_tokens":171318,"cache_creation_input_tokens":401,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":401},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CajexQfZMKNyG52pyeP9k","type":"assistant","uuid":"65679960-2d87-4f68-b783-fc9df9076d80","timestamp":"2026-05-05T15:25:48.435Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Generalizable: applies to any deterministic hook whose contract is \"stimulus → state mutation / decision\".","+","+**Auto-detect**: partial — hook-firing-counter surfaces silent-≥7d hooks as priority queue; firing-test ABSENCE for active hooks remains a gap (T6 self-scan future work per Phase 3.5 plan 010).","+","+**Where tracked**:","+- This entry (L-S59-1)","+- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/{session-end-checklist-linter,project-md-staleness-check,tool-call-first-lint,post-tool-citation-grep,budget-watchdog}-fire-test.sh` (5 NEW; 6 TCs each)","+- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log`","+","+---","+","+### L-S59-2 — Two-stage grep beats single-regex `.*` chaining for multi-substring log assertions","+**Date**: 2026-05-05","+**Context**: S59 firing-test for tool-call-first-lint hit pre-deploy iteration-bug at TC5 — assertion `grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true.*tc5-session\"` failed because real log line is `[TS=...] [session=tc5-session] mode_a_suspected=true ...` (session ID precedes classification keyword).","+","+**Rule**: When asserting that a log line contains MULTIPLE substrings, use **two-stage grep**:","+```bash","+grep \"anchor=$VAL\" \"$LOG\" | grep -q \"$keyword\"","+```","+NOT single-regex `.*` chaining:","+```bash","+grep -q \"$keyword.*anchor=$VAL\" \"$LOG\"  # Order-dependent; brittle","+```","+","+**Why**:","+- Two-stage grep is order-independent: works regardless of which substring appears first.","+- Single-regex `.*` chaining requires the writer of the assertion to know the EXACT order in which the LOG-WRITER emitted substrings. The two are usually different humans (or the same human at different times), and ordering drift is undetected at code-review time.","+- Generalizable to ANY log-line assertion: emit_warning() functions in different hooks order substrings differently; assertion-writer should not be coupled.","+","+**How to apply**:","+- Extract a helper function for repeated assertion patterns: e.g. `session_classified_mode_a() { grep \"session=$1\" \"$LOG\" | grep -q \"mode_a_suspected=true\"; }`.","+- For one-off assertions, write two-stage grep directly.","+","+**Validation**: TC5 of tool-call-first-lint-fire-test.sh — pre-deploy single-regex assertion FAILED on real log line; refactored to `session_classified_mode_a()` helper applied uniformly to TC3/TC4/TC5/TC6 → all 4 TCs PASS. Caught at firing-test stage = SUCCESS path of L-S51-1 per L-S52-3 doctrine; NOT catalogued as M-S59-X.","+","+**Severity**: low — narrowly applicable to log-line assertion authoring. But codifying prevents the same iteration-bug class in the next ~16 T7 retrofit firing-tests.","+","+**Auto-detect**: none — discovered empirically on first wrong assertion regex; no static-lint detection candidate (regex-vs-actual-output is per-hook semantics).","+","+**Where tracked**:","+- This entry (L-S59-2)","+- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/tool-call-first-lint-fire-test.sh` `session_classified_mode_a()` helper (line ~58-64)","+- `agent-workspace/quality-reports/deterministic/2026-05-05-S59-T7-retrofit-batch-5-hooks.log` § Pre-deploy iteration-bug catches"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"65679960-2d87-4f68-b783-fc9df9076d80","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"5e30b5e7-808f-4748-bf30-664276fe83c2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Crucial spec point: `for ref in $(grep)` IS unsafe with pipefail+ERR-trap (cmd-sub exit propagates; `for` is NOT in exemption list). Process substitution `<(grep)` IS safe (subshell exit not propagated to parent ERR trap) but adding `\\|\\| true` is canonical doc + lint-compliant. Validated by 27 → 0 L-S48d-1 production-smoke clearance + 17/17 firing-tests + 11 surgical fixes across 6 hooks. Generalizable for any future static analysis where bash-spec ERR-trap exemption rules govern correctness. |","-| **KI-S54-1 → CLOSED** | (was low) | Check 7 narrow alt-guard whitelist (`\\|\\| true`/`\\|\\| :` only) → STRUCTURAL FIX SHIPPED S58. Internal-detail tests over-specify and break on legitimate refactors (e.g. S57 KI-S56-1 awk-range refactor → TC4 fixture update needed). Externally observable surface IS the hook's harness contract; that's exactly what regression testing should cover. Recipe: per-TC stimulus (env var / payload / fixture file) → expected externally-observable outcome (log line / marker file / stdout shape / WARN count). Validated by 5×6=30 TCs all PASS first-iteration with 0 hook source changes required. Generalizable to remaining ~16 untested hooks. |","+| **L-S59-2** | low | Two-stage grep `grep \"anchor=$VAL\" \"$LOG\" \\| grep -q \"$keyword\"` beats single-regex `.*` chaining `grep -q \"$keyword.*anchor=$VAL\"` for multi-substring log assertions. Reason: order-independent — works regardless of which substring appears first in actual log line. Single-regex `.*` requires assertion-author to know exact emit-order, undetected at code-review. Helper-function refactor (`session_classified_mode_a()`) reduces iteration-bug class. Generalizable to ANY log-line assertion in firing-tests. |"," ","-NOTE per L-S52-3 doctrine: 0 iteration-bugs caught at firing-test stage this turn.
